First, let's list the paths from `Q` to `N`:

1. `Q -> H -> N`
2. `Q -> I -> H -> N`
3. `Q -> S -> B -> T -> C -> G -> N`
4. `Q -> H -> I -> B -> F -> E -> S -> B -> T -> C -> G -> N`
5. `Q -> J -> I -> B -> F -> E -> S -> B -> T -> C -> G -> N`

Considering all these paths, let's find the minimal set of edges to disconnect `Q` and `N`.

- E -> F -> B
- E -> B

- B -> G -> H

- E -> B -> G -> H
